+# Copie para .env.local (ignorado pelo git) e preencha:
+#   cp .env.example .env.local
+# Depois reinicie o Expo: npx expo start -c
+#
+# Sem estas variáveis o app roda 100% offline (SQLite + dados de demonstração).
+# Valores em: Supabase > Project Settings > API.
+
+# Project URL
+EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://SEU-PROJETO.supabase.co
+
+# Chave "anon / public". NUNCA use a service_role aqui: ela ignora a RLS
+# e tudo com prefixo EXPO_PUBLIC_ vai para dentro do bundle do app.
+EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8c60216
--- /dev/null
+++ b/__tests__/application/PhotoEditAndAvatar.test.ts

+import { AnimalPhoto } from '../../src/domain/entities/AnimalPhoto';
+import { User } from '../../src/domain/entities/User';
+import { ContactInfo } from '../../src/domain/value-objects/ContactInfo';
+import { UpdateAnimalUseCase } from '../../src/application/use-cases/UpdateAnimalUseCase';
+import { UpdateProfileUseCase } from '../../src/application/use-cases/UpdateProfileUseCase';
+import { AnimalRepositoryFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/AnimalRepositoryFake';
+import { SyncQueueRepositoryFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/SyncQueueRepositoryFake';
+import { AuthGatewayFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/AuthGatewayFake';
+import { UserRepositoryFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/UserRepositoryFake';
+import { PhotoStorageFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/PhotoStorageFake';
+import { makeAnimal, makePhoto } from '../../test-support/fixtures';
+
+describe('Edição do anúncio com troca de foto', () => {
+  it('a foto nova substitui a antiga, sobe a versão e entra na fila', async () => {
+    const repo = new AnimalRepositoryFake([makeAnimal({ photos: [makePhoto({ id: 'old' })] })]);
+    const queue = new SyncQueueRepositoryFake();
+    const useCase = new UpdateAnimalUseCase(repo, queue);
+    const next = new AnimalPhoto({ id: 'new', animalId: 'animal_1', localPath: 'file:///app/new.webp' });
+
+    const updated = await useCase.execute('user_1', 'animal_1', { photo: next }, false);
+
+    expect(updated.photos.map((p) => p.id)).toEqual(['new']);
+    expect(updated.version).toBe(2);
+    expect(await queue.listPending()).toHaveLength(1);
+  });
+
+  it('só o responsável troca a foto; foto de outro animal é recusada', () => {
+    const animal = makeAnimal();
+    const photo = new AnimalPhoto({ id: 'p', animalId: 'animal_1', localPath: 'file:///p.webp' });
+    expect(() => animal.replacePhotos('outro', photo)).toThrow('Somente o responsável pode alterar as fotos.');
+    const alheia = new AnimalPhoto({ id: 'q', animalId: 'animal_9', localPath: 'file:///q.webp' });
+    expect(() => animal.replacePhotos('user_1', alheia)).toThrow('Foto não pertence a este animal.');
+  });
+});
+
+describe('Foto de perfil no Storage (UpdateProfileUseCase)', () => {
+  const base = () =>
+    new User({ id: 'u1', name: 'Ana', contactInfo: new ContactInfo('ana@x.com'), role: 'USER' });
+
+  function setup(remote?: PhotoStorageFake) {
+    const gateway = new AuthGatewayFake();
+    const local = new PhotoStorageFake();
+    return {
+      gateway,
+      local,
+      useCase: new UpdateProfileUseCase(gateway, new UserRepositoryFake(), local, remote),
+    };
+  }
+
+  it('com backend: envia a foto e guarda a URL pública (visível em outro aparelho)', async () => {
+    const remote = new PhotoStorageFake();
+    const { useCase, gateway } = setup(remote);
+    const spy = jest.spyOn(gateway, 'updateProfile');
+
+    const { user } = await useCase.execute(base(), { avatarUri: 'file:///cache/a.webp' });
+
+    expect(user.avatarUri).toBe('file:///cache/a.webp');
+    expect(user.avatarUrl).toMatch(/^https:\/\/storage\.fake\/user:u1\//);
+    expect(spy.mock.calls[0][0].avatarUrl).toBe(user.avatarUrl);
+  });
+
+  it('sem backend ou com upload falhando: perfil salva e a foto segue só local', async () => {
+    const semBackend = setup();
+    const a = await semBackend.useCase.execute(base(), { avatarUri: 'file:///cache/a.webp' });
+    expect(a.user.avatarUrl).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(a.user.avatarUri).toBeDefined();
+
+    const remote = new PhotoStorageFake();
+    jest.spyOn(remote, 'uploadPending').mockRejectedValue(new Error('rede'));
+    const comFalha = setup(remote);
+    const b = await comFalha.useCase.execute(base(), { avatarUri: 'file:///cache/b.webp' });
+    expect(b.user.avatarUrl).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(b.syncedRemotely).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it('próximo salvamento reenvia uma foto que ficou só local', async () => {
+    const remote = new PhotoStorageFake();
+    const { useCase } = setup(remote);
+    const first = base().withProfile({ avatarUri: 'file:///cache/c.webp' }); // sem avatarUrl
+    const { user } = await useCase.execute(first, { bio: 'oi' });
+    expect(user.avatarUrl).toBeDefined();
+  });
+
+  it('remover a foto limpa a cópia local e a URL', async () => {
+    const remote = new PhotoStorageFake();
+    const { useCase } = setup(remote);
+    const withPhoto = base().withProfile({ avatarUri: 'file:///cache/d.webp', avatarUrl: 'https://x/d.webp' });
+    const { user } = await useCase.execute(withPhoto, { avatarUri: null });
+    expect(user.avatarUri).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(user.avatarUrl).toBeUndefined();
+  });
+
+  it('trocar a foto invalida a URL antiga e sobe a nova', async () => {
+    const remote = new PhotoStorageFake();
+    const { useCase } = setup(remote);
+    const withPhoto = base().withProfile({ avatarUri: 'file:///cache/old.webp', avatarUrl: 'https://x/old.webp' });
+    const { user } = await useCase.execute(withPhoto, { avatarUri: 'file:///cache/new.webp' });
+    expect(user.avatarUrl).not.toBe('https://x/old.webp');
+    expect(user.avatarUrl).toBeDefined();
+  });
+});
diff --git a/__tests__/application/UpdateProfile.test.ts b/__tests__/application/UpdateProfile.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8b03f94

+++ b/__tests__/application/UpdateProfile.test.ts

+import { User } from '../../src/domain/entities/User';
+import { ContactInfo } from '../../src/domain/value-objects/ContactInfo';
+import { MAX_BIO_LENGTH, normalizeInterests } from '../../src/domain/value-objects/UserProfile';
+import { UpdateProfileUseCase } from '../../src/application/use-cases/UpdateProfileUseCase';
+import { AuthGatewayFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/AuthGatewayFake';
+import { UserRepositoryFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/UserRepositoryFake';
+import { PhotoStorageFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/PhotoStorageFake';
+
+const base = () =>
+  new User({ id: 'u1', name: 'Ana', contactInfo: new ContactInfo('ana@x.com', '11999'), role: 'USER' });
+
+describe('User.withProfile / UserProfile', () => {
+  it('altera só o perfil e preserva identidade (id, e-mail, papel)', () => {
+    const u = base().withProfile({
+      name: ' Ana Maria ',
+      phone: ' 11888 ',
+      bio: ' amo gatos ',
+      interests: ['Gatos', 'Inexistente', 'Gatos', 'Cães'],
+    });
+    expect(u.id).toBe('u1');
+    expect(u.role).toBe('USER');
+    expect(u.contactInfo.email).toBe('ana@x.com');
+    expect(u.name).toBe('Ana Maria');
+    expect(u.contactInfo.phone).toBe('11888');
+    expect(u.bio).toBe('amo gatos');
+    expect(u.interests).toEqual(['Cães', 'Gatos']);
+  });
+
+  it('telefone vazio remove; avatar null remove; undefined mantém', () => {
+    const withAvatar = base().withProfile({ avatarUri: 'file://a.jpg' });
+    expect(withAvatar.avatarUri).toBe('file://a.jpg');
+    expect(withAvatar.withProfile({ name: 'Novo' }).avatarUri).toBe('file://a.jpg');
+    expect(withAvatar.withProfile({ avatarUri: null }).avatarUri).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(base().withProfile({ phone: '  ' }).contactInfo.phone).toBeUndefined();
+  });
+
+  it('rejeita nome vazio e descrição acima do limite', () => {
+    expect(() => base().withProfile({ name: '  ' })).toThrow('Nome do usuário é obrigatório.');
+    expect(() => base().withProfile({ bio: 'x'.repeat(MAX_BIO_LENGTH + 1) })).toThrow(
+      `no máximo ${MAX_BIO_LENGTH} caracteres`
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('normalizeInterests tolera nulos', () => {
+    expect(normalizeInterests(undefined)).toEqual([]);
+    expect(normalizeInterests(null)).toEqual([]);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('UpdateProfileUseCase', () => {
+  function setup() {
+    const gateway = new AuthGatewayFake();
+    const repo = new UserRepositoryFake();
+    const storage = new PhotoStorageFake();
+    return { gateway, repo, storage, useCase: new UpdateProfileUseCase(gateway, repo, storage) };
+  }
+
+  it('grava localmente e no servidor', async () => {
+    const { gateway, repo, useCase } = setup();
+    const res = await useCase.execute(gateway.mockUsers[0], { bio: 'oi', interests: ['Cães'] });
+    expect(res.syncedRemotely).toBe(true);
+    expect((await repo.findById('usr_1'))?.bio).toBe('oi');
+    expect(gateway.mockUsers[0].interests).toEqual(['Cães']);
+  });
+
+  it('servidor indisponível → mantém a edição local e avisa (sem perda silenciosa)', async () => {
+    const { gateway, repo, useCase } = setup();
+    gateway.updateProfile = jest.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error('network'));
+    const res = await useCase.execute(gateway.mockUsers[0], { bio: 'offline' });
+    expect(res.syncedRemotely).toBe(false);
+    expect(res.user.bio).toBe('offline');
+    expect((await repo.findById('usr_1'))?.bio).toBe('offline');
+  });
+
+  it('dados inválidos não gravam nada', async () => {
+    const { gateway, repo, useCase } = setup();
+    await expect(useCase.execute(gateway.mockUsers[0], { name: '' })).rejects.toThrow();
+    expect(await repo.findById('usr_1')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('foto: persiste pelo PhotoStorage e usa o destino definitivo; remover limpa', async () => {
+    const { gateway, useCase, storage } = setup();
+    const spy = jest.spyOn(storage, 'persistLocal');
+    const first = await useCase.execute(gateway.mockUsers[0], { avatarUri: 'file://tmp/cam.jpg' });
+    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(first.user.avatarUri).toBeDefined();
+
+    // Mesmo URI de novo: nada a persistir.
+    await useCase.execute(first.user, { avatarUri: first.user.avatarUri });
+    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+    const removed = await useCase.execute(first.user, { avatarUri: null });
+    expect(removed.user.avatarUri).toBeUndefined();
+  });
+
+  it('sem PhotoStorage guarda a URI como veio', async () => {
+    const gateway = new AuthGatewayFake();
+    const useCase = new UpdateProfileUseCase(gateway, new UserRepositoryFake());
+    const res = await useCase.execute(gateway.mockUsers[0], { avatarUri: 'file://x.jpg' });
+    expect(res.user.avatarUri).toBe('file://x.jpg');
+  });
+});
diff --git a/__tests__/domain/AccessPolicy.test.ts b/__tests__/domain/AccessPolicy.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..dfb2567

+++ b/__tests__/domain/AccessPolicy.test.ts

+import { canAccess, isAuthenticatedRole, requiredAccessLevel, AppArea } from '../../src/domain/services/AccessPolicy';
+import {
+  validateLogin,
+  validateRegistration,
+  isValidEmail,
+  MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH,
+} from '../../src/domain/services/CredentialPolicy';
+
+describe('AccessPolicy (doc §1.1 / §15.1)', () => {
+  const publicAreas: AppArea[] = ['SEARCH', 'ANIMAL_DETAILS'];
+  const authAreas: AppArea[] = ['FAVORITES', 'EXPRESS_INTEREST', 'MY_ANIMALS', 'MANAGE_ANIMAL', 'SYNC'];
+
+  it('visitante (null/undefined/VISITOR) só acessa áreas públicas', () => {
+    for (const role of [null, undefined, 'VISITOR' as const]) {
+      publicAreas.forEach((a) => expect(canAccess(role, a)).toBe(true));
+      [...authAreas, 'MODERATION' as const].forEach((a) => expect(canAccess(role, a)).toBe(false));
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('qualquer usuário autenticado acessa áreas autenticadas, mas não a moderação', () => {
+    for (const role of ['USER', 'RESPONSIBLE', 'INTERESTED'] as const) {
+      [...publicAreas, ...authAreas].forEach((a) => expect(canAccess(role, a)).toBe(true));
+      expect(canAccess(role, 'MODERATION')).toBe(false);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('administrador acessa tudo', () => {
+    [...publicAreas, ...authAreas, 'MODERATION' as const].forEach((a) =>
+      expect(canAccess('ADMIN', a)).toBe(true)
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('expõe nível exigido e papel autenticado', () => {
+    expect(requiredAccessLevel('SEARCH')).toBe('PUBLIC');
+    expect(requiredAccessLevel('FAVORITES')).toBe('AUTHENTICATED');
+    expect(requiredAccessLevel('MODERATION')).toBe('ADMIN');
+    expect(isAuthenticatedRole('USER')).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAuthenticatedRole('VISITOR')).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAuthenticatedRole(null)).toBe(false);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('CredentialPolicy', () => {
+  it('valida e-mail', () => {
+    expect(isValidEmail(' a@b.co ')).toBe(true);
+    expect(isValidEmail('a@b')).toBe(false);
+    expect(isValidEmail('sem arroba')).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it('login: exige campos e e-mail válido, sem tamanho mínimo de senha', () => {
+    expect(validateLogin('', '')).toBe('E-mail e senha são obrigatórios.');
+    expect(validateLogin('x', '1')).toBe('Informe um e-mail válido.');
+    expect(validateLogin('a@b.co', '1')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('cadastro: devolve a primeira violação', () => {
+    const ok = { name: 'Ana', email: 'a@b.co', password: '123456', confirmPassword: '123456' };
+    expect(validateRegistration(ok)).toBeNull();
+    expect(validateRegistration({ ...ok, name: ' ' })).toBe('Nome é obrigatório.');
+    expect(validateRegistration({ ...ok, email: '' })).toBe('E-mail é obrigatório.');
+    expect(validateRegistration({ ...ok, email: 'x' })).toBe('Informe um e-mail válido.');
+    expect(validateRegistration({ ...ok, password: '', confirmPassword: '' })).toBe('Senha é obrigatória.');
+    expect(validateRegistration({ ...ok, password: '123', confirmPassword: '123' })).toBe(
+      `A senha deve ter ao menos ${MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH} caracteres.`
+    );
+    expect(validateRegistration({ ...ok, confirmPassword: 'outra1' })).toBe('As senhas não coincidem.');
+  });
+});

+/**
+ * @jest-environment node
+ */
+import fs from 'fs';
+import os from 'os';
+import path from 'path';
+import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
+import { BetterSqliteDriver } from '../../test-support/BetterSqliteDriver';
+import { FakeSupabaseClient, FakeSupabaseDb, seedFakeDb } from '../../test-support/SupabaseFake';
+import { loadAppContainer, AppContainer } from '../../src/infrastructure/composition/loadAppContainer';
+import { getFullSync } from '../../src/adapters/hooks/useFullSync';
+import { ImageProcessorFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/ImageProcessorFake';
+import { CreateAnimalUseCase } from '../../src/application/use-cases/CreateAnimalUseCase';
+import { UpdateAnimalUseCase } from '../../src/application/use-cases/UpdateAnimalUseCase';
+import { MarkAnimalAdoptedUseCase } from '../../src/application/use-cases/MarkAnimalAdoptedUseCase';
+import { ResolveConflictUseCase } from '../../src/application/use-cases/ResolveConflictUseCase';
+import { Animal } from '../../src/domain/entities/Animal';
+import { AnimalPhoto } from '../../src/domain/entities/AnimalPhoto';
+import { AnimalCharacteristics } from '../../src/domain/value-objects/AnimalCharacteristics';
+import { ApproximateLocation } from '../../src/domain/value-objects/ApproximateLocation';
+import { newUuid } from '../../src/domain/services/IdGenerator';
+
+/**
+ * E2E §16.4 itens 4–6 e 8 com SQLite em ARQUIVO (fechar/reabrir de verdade)
+ * e o backend simulado com as mesmas regras do `supabase/init.sql`.
+ */
+const OWNER = '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111';
+const STRANGER = '99999999-9999-4999-8999-999999999999';
+
+describe('E2E offline → sync (doc §16.4 itens 4, 5, 6 e 8)', () => {
+  let directory: string;
+  let databasePath: string;
+  let db: FakeSupabaseDb;
+  const opened: BetterSqliteDriver[] = [];
+
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    directory = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'enlace-e2e-'));
+    databasePath = path.join(directory, 'enlace.db');
+    db = seedFakeDb({ users: [] });
+  });
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    opened.splice(0).forEach((d) => d.close());
+    fs.rmSync(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  });
+
+  /** "Abre o app": novo container sobre o MESMO arquivo, com a sessão do usuário. */
+  async function openApp(userId: string): Promise<AppContainer> {
+    const driver = new BetterSqliteDriver(new Database(databasePath));
+    opened.push(driver);
+    const client = new FakeSupabaseClient({ context: { db, currentUserId: userId, isAdmin: false } });
+    return loadAppContainer(driver, {
+      supabaseClient: client,
+      imageProcessor: new ImageProcessorFake(),
+      readFile: async () => new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3]),
+    });
+  }
+
+  function closeApp() {
+    opened.splice(0).forEach((d) => d.close());
+  }
+
+  function newAnimal(photo = false): Animal {
+    const id = newUuid();
+    const animal = new Animal({
+      id,
+      ownerId: OWNER,
+      name: 'Mel',
+      characteristics: new AnimalCharacteristics({ species: 'Gato', size: 'Pequeno', approximateAge: '1 ano', sex: 'Fêmea' }),
+      location: new ApproximateLocation({ latitude: -21.55, longitude: -45.43, neighborhood: 'Centro', city: 'Varginha', region: 'MG' }),
+    });
+    if (photo) animal.addPhoto(new AnimalPhoto({ id: newUuid(), animalId: id, localPath: 'file:///app/mel.webp' }));
+    return animal;
+  }
+
+  it('item 4: cria offline → fecha o app → reabre → sincroniza (anúncio e foto chegam ao servidor)', async () => {
+    let app = await openApp(OWNER);
+    const animal = newAnimal(true);
+    const created = await new CreateAnimalUseCase(app.animalRepository, app.syncQueueRepository, app.unitOfWork).execute(animal, false);
+    expect(created.synced).toBe(false);
+    expect(db.animals).toHaveLength(0);
+
+    closeApp();
+    app = await openApp(OWNER); // reabre
+
+    expect(await app.syncQueueRepository.listPending()).toHaveLength(1); // fila intacta
+    expect((await app.animalRepository.findById(animal.id))?.name).toBe('Mel'); // cache intacto
+
+    const result = await getFullSync(app).execute();
+
+    expect(result.push).toMatchObject({ successCount: 1, failedCount: 0 });
+    expect(db.animals.map((a) => a.id)).toContain(animal.id);
+    expect(db['animal_photos']).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(await app.syncQueueRepository.listPending()).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+
+  it('item 5: edita offline → fecha e reabre → a edição chega ao servidor', async () => {
+    let app = await openApp(OWNER);
+    const animal = newAnimal();
+    await new CreateAnimalUseCase(app.animalRepository, app.syncQueueRepository, app.unitOfWork).execute(animal, true);
+    expect(db.animals).toHaveLength(1);
+
+    await new UpdateAnimalUseCase(app.animalRepository, app.syncQueueRepository, app.unitOfWork).execute(OWNER, animal.id, { name: 'Mel Editada' }, false);
+    expect(db.animals[0].name).toBe('Mel');
+
+    closeApp();
+    app = await openApp(OWNER);
+    const result = await getFullSync(app).execute();
+
+    expect(result.push.successCount).toBe(1);
+    expect(db.animals[0].name).toBe('Mel Editada');
+  });
+
+  it('item 6: conflito (situação muda nos dois lados) gera revisão e a escolha do responsável vale', async () => {
+    let app = await openApp(OWNER);
+    const animal = newAnimal();
+    await new CreateAnimalUseCase(app.animalRepository, app.syncQueueRepository, app.unitOfWork).execute(animal, true);
+
+    await new MarkAnimalAdoptedUseCase(app.animalRepository, app.syncQueueRepository, app.unitOfWork).execute(OWNER, animal.id, false);
+    // Enquanto isso o servidor muda por outro caminho.
+    db.animals[0] = { ...db.animals[0], name: 'Mudou no servidor', version: db.animals[0].version + 1 };
+
+    closeApp();
+    app = await openApp(OWNER);
+    const first = await getFullSync(app).execute();
+    expect(first.push.conflictCount).toBe(1);
+    expect(db.animals[0].status).toBe('AVAILABLE');
+
+    const resolver = new ResolveConflictUseCase(app.animalRepository, app.remoteAnimalSource!, app.syncQueueRepository);
+    const [conflict] = await resolver.listConflicts();
+    await resolver.keepLocal(conflict.action.id);
+    const second = await getFullSync(app).execute();
+
+    expect(second.push.successCount).toBe(1);
+    expect(db.animals[0].status).toBe('ADOPTED');
+  });
+
+  it('item 8: terceiro não edita nem exclui anúncio alheio — nem localmente, nem no servidor (RLS)', async () => {
+    let app = await openApp(OWNER);
+    const animal = newAnimal();
+    await new CreateAnimalUseCase(app.animalRepository, app.syncQueueRepository, app.unitOfWork).execute(animal, true);
+    closeApp();
+
+    app = await openApp(STRANGER);
+    await getFullSync(app).execute(); // traz o anúncio do dono
+    const edit = new UpdateAnimalUseCase(app.animalRepository, app.syncQueueRepository, app.unitOfWork);
+    await expect(edit.execute(STRANGER, animal.id, { name: 'Invasor' }, true)).rejects.toThrow(
+      'Somente o responsável pelo anúncio pode alterar seus dados.'
+    );
+
+    // Mesmo contornando o domínio, a "RLS" do servidor recusa o UPDATE.
+    const local = (await app.animalRepository.findById(animal.id))!;
+    await expect(app.animalRepository.updateRemote(local)).rejects.toThrow();
+    expect(db.animals[0].name).toBe('Mel');
+  });
+});

+import React from 'react';
+import { Text } from 'react-native';
+import { render, waitFor, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react-native';
+
+const mockReplace = jest.fn();
+jest.mock('expo-router', () => {
+  const { Text: T } = require('react-native');
+  return {
+    useRouter: () => ({ replace: mockReplace, push: jest.fn() }),
+    Redirect: ({ href }: { href: string }) => <T testID="redirect">{href}</T>,
+  };
+});
+
+import { AuthProvider } from '../../src/adapters/context/AuthContext';
+import { RequireAccess } from '../../src/adapters/guards/RequireAccess';
+import { AccountScreen } from '../../src/adapters/screens/AccountScreen';
+import { AuthGatewayFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/AuthGatewayFake';
+import { SessionStorageFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/SessionStorageFake';
+
+// O fake restaura mockUsers[0] (Ana, RESPONSIBLE) para qualquer token salvo.
+async function withSession(kind: 'ana' | 'admin' | 'visitor') {
+  const gateway = new AuthGatewayFake();
+  const storage = new SessionStorageFake();
+  if (kind === 'ana') await storage.saveSessionToken('t');
+  if (kind === 'admin') {
+    await storage.saveSessionToken('t');
+    gateway.mockUsers = [gateway.mockUsers[2]];
+  }
+  return { gateway, storage };
+}
+
+describe('RequireAccess (guarda de rotas)', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => mockReplace.mockClear());
+
+  it('visitante em área restrita → redireciona para /login', async () => {
+    const { gateway, storage } = await withSession('visitor');
+    const { getByTestId, queryByText } = await render(
+      <AuthProvider authGateway={gateway} sessionStorage={storage}>
+        <RequireAccess area="FAVORITES">{() => <Text>segredo</Text>}</RequireAccess>
+      </AuthProvider>
+    );
+    await waitFor(() => expect(getByTestId('redirect').props.children).toBe('/login'));
+    expect(queryByText('segredo')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('autenticado acessa área autenticada e recebe o usuário', async () => {
+    const { gateway, storage } = await withSession('ana');
+    const { findByText } = await render(
+      <AuthProvider authGateway={gateway} sessionStorage={storage}>
+        <RequireAccess area="MY_ANIMALS">{(u) => <Text>olá {u.name}</Text>}</RequireAccess>
+      </AuthProvider>
+    );
+    expect(await findByText('olá Ana Silva')).toBeTruthy();
+  });
+
+  it('usuário comum não entra na moderação (acesso negado); admin entra', async () => {
+    const ana = await withSession('ana');
+    const denied = await render(
+      <AuthProvider authGateway={ana.gateway} sessionStorage={ana.storage}>
+        <RequireAccess area="MODERATION">{() => <Text>painel</Text>}</RequireAccess>
+      </AuthProvider>
+    );
+    expect(await denied.findByTestId('access-denied')).toBeTruthy();
+    await fireEvent.press(denied.getByTestId('access-denied-back'));
+    expect(mockReplace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/(tabs)');
+    denied.unmount();
+
+    const adm = await withSession('admin');
+    const allowed = await render(
+      <AuthProvider authGateway={adm.gateway} sessionStorage={adm.storage}>
+        <RequireAccess area="MODERATION">{() => <Text>painel</Text>}</RequireAccess>
+      </AuthProvider>
+    );
+    expect(await allowed.findByText('painel')).toBeTruthy();
+  });
+});
+
+describe('AccountScreen', () => {
+  it('visitante vê convite para entrar/criar conta', async () => {
+    const onLogin = jest.fn();
+    const onRegister = jest.fn();
+    const { getByTestId } = await render(
+      <AccountScreen user={null} onLogin={onLogin} onRegister={onRegister} />
+    );
+    await fireEvent.press(getByTestId('account-login'));
+    await fireEvent.press(getByTestId('account-register'));
+    expect(onLogin).toHaveBeenCalled();
+    expect(onRegister).toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('autenticado vê perfil, papel e consegue sair; carregando mostra spinner', async () => {
+    const gateway = new AuthGatewayFake();
+    const onLogout = jest.fn();
+    const { getByTestId } = await render(<AccountScreen user={gateway.mockUsers[0]} onLogout={onLogout} />);
+    expect(getByTestId('account-name').props.children).toBe('Ana Silva');
+    expect(getByTestId('account-role').props.children).toBe('Responsável');
+    await fireEvent.press(getByTestId('account-logout'));
+    expect(onLogout).toHaveBeenCalled();
+
+    const loading = await render(<AccountScreen user={null} isLoading />);
+    expect(loading.getByTestId('account-loading')).toBeTruthy();
+  });
+});

+describe('SupabaseAuthGateway.register (signUp e-mail/senha)', () => {
+  function gatewayWith(signUp: SupabaseAuthApi['signUp']) {
+    const client: SupabaseClientLike = new FakeSupabaseClient({
+      context: { db: { animals: [], users: [] }, currentUserId: null, isAdmin: false },
+      authHandlers: { signUp },
+    });
+    return { gw: new SupabaseAuthGateway({ client }), client };
+  }
+
+  it('envia nome/telefone como metadata e nunca o papel', async () => {
+    const signUp = jest.fn(async () => ({
+      data: {
+        user: { id: 'u-1', email: 'maria@test.com' },
+        session: { access_token: 't', user: { id: 'u-1' } },
+      },
+      error: null,
+    }));
+    const { gw } = gatewayWith(signUp);
+
+    const res = await gw.register({

+
+    expect(signUp).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
+      email: 'maria@test.com',
+      password: 'senha123',
+      options: { data: { name: 'Maria', phone: '35 99999-0000' } },
+    });
+    expect(res.user.role).toBe('USER');
+    expect(res.user.name).toBe('Maria');
+    expect(res.needsEmailConfirmation).toBe(false);
+  });

+  it('sem sessão → pede confirmação de e-mail', async () => {
+    const { gw } = gatewayWith(async () => ({
+      data: { user: { id: 'u-2', email: 'a@test.com' }, session: null },
+      error: null,
+    }));
+    const res = await gw.register({ name: 'A', email: 'a@test.com', password: 'senha123' });
+    expect(res.needsEmailConfirmation).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it('e-mail já cadastrado (erro ou identities vazio) → mensagem amigável', async () => {
+    const dup = gatewayWith(async () => ({
+      data: { user: null, session: null },
+      error: { message: 'User already registered' },
+    }));
+    await expect(
+      dup.gw.register({ name: 'A', email: 'a@test.com', password: 'senha123' })
+    ).rejects.toThrow('E-mail já cadastrado.');
+
+    const silent = gatewayWith(async () => ({
+      data: { user: { id: 'u-3', email: 'a@test.com', identities: [] } as any, session: null },
+      error: null,
+    }));
+    await expect(
+      silent.gw.register({ name: 'A', email: 'a@test.com', password: 'senha123' })
+    ).rejects.toThrow('E-mail já cadastrado.');
+  });
+
+  it('senha fraca, sem usuário e rede fora → erros tratados', async () => {
+    await expect(
+      gatewayWith(async () => ({
+        data: { user: null, session: null },
+        error: { message: 'Password should be at least 6 characters' },
+      })).gw.register({ name: 'A', email: 'a@test.com', password: '1' })
+    ).rejects.toThrow('Senha muito fraca');
+
+    await expect(
+      gatewayWith(async () => ({ data: { user: null, session: null }, error: null })).gw.register({
+        name: 'A',
+        email: 'a@test.com',
+        password: 'senha123',
+      })
+    ).rejects.toThrow('Não foi possível concluir o cadastro.');
+  });
+});
+
+describe('SupabaseAuthGateway.updateProfile (RLS users_update_own)', () => {
+  const { User } = require('../../../src/domain/entities/User');
+  const { ContactInfo } = require('../../../src/domain/value-objects/ContactInfo');
+  const me = () =>
+    new User({
+      id: 'u-1',
+      name: 'Ana Maria',
+      contactInfo: new ContactInfo('ana@test.com', '11888'),
+      bio: 'Amo gatos',
+      interests: ['Gatos'],
+    });
+
+  function gatewayFor(currentUserId: string | null, db = { animals: [], users: [{ id: 'u-1', name: 'Ana', user_type: 'USER' }] }) {
+    const client: SupabaseClientLike = new FakeSupabaseClient({
+      context: { db, currentUserId, isAdmin: false },
+    });
+    return { gw: new SupabaseAuthGateway({ client }), db };
+  }
+
+  it('grava nome, telefone, descrição e interesses na própria linha — sem tocar no papel', async () => {
+    const { gw, db } = gatewayFor('u-1');
+    await gw.updateProfile(me());
+    expect(db.users[0]).toMatchObject({
+      name: 'Ana Maria',
+      phone: '11888',
+      bio: 'Amo gatos',
+      interests: ['Gatos'],
+      user_type: 'USER',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('terceiro/anônimo: RLS devolve zero linhas → erro e nada é gravado', async () => {
+    for (const uid of ['outro', null]) {
+      const { gw, db } = gatewayFor(uid);
+      await expect(gw.updateProfile(me())).rejects.toThrow('Não foi possível salvar o perfil no servidor.');
+      expect(db.users[0]).toMatchObject({ name: 'Ana' });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('erro do servidor e falha de rede viram mensagem amigável', async () => {
+    const failing = (impl: () => any): SupabaseAuthGateway =>
+      new SupabaseAuthGateway({
+        client: {
+          auth: {} as any,
+          from: () => ({ update: () => ({ eq: () => ({ select: impl }) }) }) as any,
+        } as unknown as SupabaseClientLike,
+      });
+    await expect(
+      failing(() => Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: { message: 'Failed to fetch' } })).updateProfile(me())
+    ).rejects.toThrow('Sem conexão com o servidor.');
+    await expect(
+      failing(() => Promise.reject(new Error('network down'))).updateProfile(me())
+    ).rejects.toThrow('Sem conexão com o servidor.');
+  });
+
+  it('avatar_url é gravado e lido no perfil', async () => {
+    const { gw, db } = gatewayFor('u-1');
+    await gw.updateProfile(me().withProfile({ avatarUrl: 'https://cdn/a.webp' }));
+    expect(db.users[0]).toMatchObject({ avatar_url: 'https://cdn/a.webp' });
+    await gw.updateProfile(me());
+    expect(db.users[0]).toMatchObject({ avatar_url: null });
+  });
+});

+import { AnimalPhoto } from '../../../src/domain/entities/AnimalPhoto';
+import { PhotoStorageFake } from '../../../src/application/fakes/PhotoStorageFake';
+import {
+  SupabasePhotoStorage,
+  PhotoUploadError,
+  photoObjectPath,
+  avatarObjectPath,
+  PHOTO_BUCKET,
+} from '../../../src/infrastructure/supabase/storage/SupabasePhotoStorage';
+import { FakeSupabaseClient, FAKE_BUCKET_MAX_BYTES } from '../../../test-support/SupabaseFake';
+
+const UID = '3e0d7a30-1111-4444-8888-999999999999';
+
+function setup(
+  currentUserId: string | null = UID,
+  readFile: (uri: string) => Promise<Uint8Array> = async () => new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3])
+) {
+  const client = new FakeSupabaseClient({
+    context: { db: { animals: [], users: [] }, currentUserId, isAdmin: false },
+  });
+  const local = new PhotoStorageFake();
+  const storage = new SupabasePhotoStorage({ local, client, readFile });
+  return { client, local, storage };
+}
+
+const webp = (over: Partial<ConstructorParameters<typeof AnimalPhoto>[0]> = {}) =>
+  new AnimalPhoto({ id: 'photo-1', animalId: 'animal-1', localPath: 'file:///app/photo-1.webp', ...over });
+
+describe('SupabasePhotoStorage (bucket animal-photos)', () => {
+  it('envia o WebP para {uid}/{animal}/{foto}.webp e registra a URL pública', async () => {
+    const { client, storage, local } = setup();
+    const result = await storage.uploadPending(webp());
+
+    const path = photoObjectPath(UID, 'animal-1', 'photo-1');
+    expect(path).toBe(`${UID}/animal-1/photo-1.webp`);
+    expect(client.storageObjects.get(path)).toMatchObject({
+      bucket: PHOTO_BUCKET,
+      contentType: 'image/webp',
+    });
+    expect(result.remoteUrl).toBe(
+      `https://fake.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/${PHOTO_BUCKET}/${path}`
+    );
+    expect(result.syncState.isCompleted()).toBe(true);
+    // O armazenamento local também registrou a URL remota.
+    expect(local.photos.get('photo-1')?.remoteUrl).toBe(result.remoteUrl);
+  });
+
+  it('reenviar uma foto já enviada é idempotente (não sobe de novo)', async () => {
+    const { client, storage } = setup();
+    const first = await storage.uploadPending(webp());
+    const spy = jest.spyOn(client.storage, 'from');
+    const second = await storage.uploadPending(first);
+    expect(second.remoteUrl).toBe(first.remoteUrl);
+    expect(spy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('recusa arquivo que não é WebP (conversão obrigatória)', async () => {
+    const { client, storage } = setup();
+    await expect(storage.uploadPending(webp({ localPath: 'file:///app/photo-1.jpg' }))).rejects.toThrow(
+      'A foto precisa estar em WebP antes do envio.'
+    );
+    expect(client.storageObjects.size).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  it('sem login → erro claro e nada enviado', async () => {
+    const { client, storage } = setup(null);
+    await expect(storage.uploadPending(webp())).rejects.toThrow('Faça login para enviar fotos ao servidor.');
+    expect(client.storageObjects.size).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  it('arquivo ilegível → erro claro e nada enviado', async () => {
+    const { client, storage } = setup(UID, async () => {
+      throw new Error('ENOENT');
+    });
+    await expect(storage.uploadPending(webp())).rejects.toThrow('Não foi possível ler o arquivo da foto.');
+    expect(client.storageObjects.size).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  it('foto sem arquivo local e sem URL remota é recusada pela própria entidade', () => {
+    expect(() => new AnimalPhoto({ id: 'p', animalId: 'a' })).toThrow();
+  });
+
+  it('RLS: o servidor recusa gravar na pasta de outro usuário', async () => {
+    const { client } = setup();
+    const res = await client.storage
+      .from(PHOTO_BUCKET)
+      .upload('outro-usuario/animal-1/photo-1.webp', new Uint8Array([1]), { contentType: 'image/webp' });
+    expect(res.error?.message).toContain('row-level security');
+    expect(client.storageObjects.size).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  it('erro devolvido pelo servidor no upload vira PhotoUploadError', async () => {
+    const { client, storage } = setup();
+    (client.storage as { from: unknown }).from = () => ({
+      upload: async () => ({ data: null, error: { message: 'row-level security' } }),
+      remove: async () => ({ error: null }),
+      getPublicUrl: () => ({ data: { publicUrl: '' } }),
+    });
+    await expect(storage.uploadPending(webp())).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(PhotoUploadError);
+  });
+
+  it('o bucket recusa arquivo acima do limite e tipo diferente de WebP', async () => {
+    const big = setup(UID, async () => new Uint8Array(FAKE_BUCKET_MAX_BYTES + 1));
+    await expect(big.storage.uploadPending(webp())).rejects.toThrow('Falha no envio da foto');
+
+    const bucket = setup().client.storage.from(PHOTO_BUCKET);
+    const res = await bucket.upload(`${UID}/a/p.jpg`, new Uint8Array([1]), { contentType: 'image/jpeg' });
+    expect(res.error?.message).toContain('not supported');
+  });
+
+  it('exceção de rede no upload vira PhotoUploadError', async () => {
+    const { client, storage } = setup();
+    (client.storage as { from: unknown }).from = () => ({
+      upload: async () => {
+        throw new Error('network down');
+      },
+      remove: async () => ({ error: null }),
+      getPublicUrl: () => ({ data: { publicUrl: '' } }),
+    });
+    await expect(storage.uploadPending(webp())).rejects.toThrow('Falha no envio da foto: network down');
+  });
+
+  it('persistência local, fila pendente e remoção delegam ao armazenamento local', async () => {
+    const { storage, local } = setup();
+    await storage.persistLocal(webp());
+    expect(await storage.listPendingUpload()).toHaveLength(1);
+    await storage.remove('photo-1');
+    expect(local.photos.has('photo-1')).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it('foto de perfil (animalId user:<id>) vai para {uid}/avatar/{foto}.webp', async () => {
+    const { client, storage } = setup();
+    const result = await storage.uploadPending(webp({ id: 'av-1', animalId: `user:${UID}` }));
+    expect(avatarObjectPath(UID, 'av-1')).toBe(`${UID}/avatar/av-1.webp`);
+    expect(client.storageObjects.has(`${UID}/avatar/av-1.webp`)).toBe(true);
+    expect(result.remoteUrl).toContain(`${UID}/avatar/av-1.webp`);
+  });
+});

index 0000000..9ee4f42

+/**
+ * @jest-environment node
+ */
+import { BetterSqliteDriver } from '../../../../test-support/BetterSqliteDriver';
+import { applyMigrations, getSchemaVersion } from '../../../../src/infrastructure/database/sqlite/migrations';
+import { SqliteUserRepository } from '../../../../src/infrastructure/database/sqlite/repositories/SqliteUserRepository';
+import { User } from '../../../../src/domain/entities/User';
+import { ContactInfo } from '../../../../src/domain/value-objects/ContactInfo';
+
+describe('Perfil do usuário no SQLite real (migração 2)', () => {
+  let driver: BetterSqliteDriver;
+
+  beforeEach(async () => {
+    driver = new BetterSqliteDriver();
+    await applyMigrations(driver);
+  });
+  afterEach(() => driver.close());
+
+  const make = (extra: Partial<ConstructorParameters<typeof User>[0]> = {}) =>
+    new User({ id: 'u1', name: 'Ana', contactInfo: new ContactInfo('ana@x.com', '11999'), ...extra });
+
+  it('a migração 2 cria as colunas de perfil', async () => {
+    expect(await getSchemaVersion(driver)).toBe(2);
+    const cols = await driver.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>('PRAGMA table_info(users)');
+    expect(cols.map((c) => c.name)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['bio', 'avatar_uri', 'interests']));
+  });
+
+  it('grava e lê descrição, foto e interesses; atualizar não duplica', async () => {
+    const repo = new SqliteUserRepository(driver);
+    await repo.save(make({ bio: 'Amo animais', avatarUri: 'file:///a.jpg', interests: ['Cães', 'Filhotes'] }));
+
+    const loaded = await repo.findById('u1');
+    expect(loaded?.bio).toBe('Amo animais');
+    expect(loaded?.avatarUri).toBe('file:///a.jpg');
+    expect(loaded?.interests).toEqual(['Cães', 'Filhotes']);
+
+    await repo.save(make({ bio: 'Mudei', interests: [] }));
+    const updated = await repo.findById('u1');
+    expect(updated?.bio).toBe('Mudei');
+    expect(updated?.avatarUri).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(updated?.interests).toEqual([]);
+    const count = await driver.getFirstAsync<{ n: number }>('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM users');
+    expect(count?.n).toBe(1);
+  });
+
+  it('interesses corrompidos no banco viram lista vazia (não quebram o app)', async () => {
+    await driver.runAsync(
+      `INSERT INTO users (id, name, email, user_type, interests, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
+      ['u2', 'Bia', 'bia@x.com', 'USER', '{lixo', new Date().toISOString()]
+    );
+    const repo = new SqliteUserRepository(driver);
+    expect((await repo.findById('u2'))?.interests).toEqual([]);
+    await driver.runAsync(`UPDATE users SET interests = ? WHERE id = ?`, ['"texto"', 'u2']);
+    expect((await repo.findById('u2'))?.interests).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it('banco legado (versão 1, sem as colunas) é migrado preservando os usuários', async () => {
+    const legacy = new BetterSqliteDriver();
+    await legacy.execAsync(
+      `CREATE TABLE users (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL, email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
+        phone TEXT, user_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER', created_at TEXT NOT NULL)`
+    );
+    await legacy.runAsync(
+      `INSERT INTO users (id, name, email, created_at) VALUES ('old', 'Velho', 'v@x.com', '2026-01-01')`
+    );
+    await legacy.execAsync('PRAGMA user_version = 1');
+    await applyMigrations(legacy);
+    expect(await getSchemaVersion(legacy)).toBe(2);
+    const user = await new SqliteUserRepository(legacy).findById('old');
+    expect(user?.name).toBe('Velho');
+    expect(user?.interests).toEqual([]);
+    legacy.close();
+  });
+});

+import { loadAppContainer } from '../../../src/infrastructure/composition/loadAppContainer';
+import { InMemorySqliteDriver } from '../../../src/infrastructure/database/sqlite/DatabaseDriver';
+import { DeleteAnimalUseCase } from '../../../src/application/use-cases/DeleteAnimalUseCase';
+import { UpdateAnimalUseCase } from '../../../src/application/use-cases/UpdateAnimalUseCase';
+import { CreateAnimalUseCase } from '../../../src/application/use-cases/CreateAnimalUseCase';
+import { ProcessSyncQueueUseCase } from '../../../src/application/use-cases/ProcessSyncQueueUseCase';
+import { ImageProcessorFake } from '../../../src/application/fakes/ImageProcessorFake';
+import { SyncAction } from '../../../src/domain/entities/SyncAction';
+import { AnimalPhoto } from '../../../src/domain/entities/AnimalPhoto';
+import { Animal } from '../../../src/domain/entities/Animal';
+import { newUuid } from '../../../src/domain/services/IdGenerator';
+import { AnimalCharacteristics } from '../../../src/domain/value-objects/AnimalCharacteristics';
+import { ApproximateLocation } from '../../../src/domain/value-objects/ApproximateLocation';
+import { FakeSupabaseClient, seedFakeDb } from '../../../test-support/SupabaseFake';
+
+const UID = '3e0d7a30-1111-4444-8888-999999999999';
+
+async function setup() {
+  const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [{ id: UID, name: 'Ana', user_type: 'USER' }] });
+  const client = new FakeSupabaseClient({ context: { db, currentUserId: UID, isAdmin: false } });
+  const container = await loadAppContainer(new InMemorySqliteDriver(), {
+    supabaseClient: client,
+    imageProcessor: new ImageProcessorFake(),
+    readFile: async () => new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3]),
+  });
+  return { db, client, container };
+}
+
+async function createOfflineAnimalWithPhoto(container: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof setup>>['container']) {
+  const animalId = newUuid();
+  const animal = new Animal({
+    id: animalId,
+    ownerId: UID,
+    name: 'Mel',
+    characteristics: new AnimalCharacteristics({
+      species: 'Gato',
+      size: 'Pequeno',
+      approximateAge: '1 ano',
+      sex: 'Fêmea',
+    }),
+    location: new ApproximateLocation({
+      latitude: -21.55,
+      longitude: -45.43,
+      neighborhood: 'Centro',
+      city: 'Varginha',
+      region: 'MG',
+    }),
+  });
+  // Captura: a conversão para WebP acontece ao persistir (pipeline obrigatório).
+  const photo = await container.photoStorage.persistLocal(
+    new AnimalPhoto({ id: newUuid(), animalId, localPath: 'file:///cache/cam.jpg' })
+  );
+  animal.addPhoto(photo);
+  await container.animalRepository.saveLocal(animal);
+  await container.syncQueueRepository.enqueue(
+    new SyncAction({
+      id: newUuid(),
+      operation: 'CREATE',
+      entityType: 'animal',
+      entityId: animalId,
+      payload: {},
+    })
+  );
+  return { animal, animalId, photo };
+}
+
+describe('Passo 4 — foto vai ao Supabase Storage antes do anúncio (doc §14.1)', () => {
+  it('sync CREATE: sobe o WebP ao bucket, registra animal_photos e guarda a URL no cache local', async () => {
+    const { db, client, container } = await setup();
+    const { animalId, photo } = await createOfflineAnimalWithPhoto(container);
+    expect(photo.localPath).toMatch(/\.webp$/);
+
+    const useCase = new ProcessSyncQueueUseCase(
+      container.syncQueueRepository,
+      container.animalRepository,
+      undefined,
+      container.remotePhotoStorage
+    );
+    const result = await useCase.execute();
+
+    expect(result).toMatchObject({ successCount: 1, failedCount: 0 });
+    const path = `${UID}/${animalId}/${photo.id}.webp`;
+    expect(client.storageObjects.get(path)).toMatchObject({ contentType: 'image/webp' });
+
+    const publicUrl = `https://fake.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/animal-photos/${path}`;
+    expect(db.animals).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(db['animal_photos']).toEqual([
+      expect.objectContaining({ id: photo.id, animal_id: animalId, remote_url: publicUrl, is_main: true }),
+    ]);
+
+    const local = await container.animalRepository.findById(animalId);
+    expect(local?.photos[0].remoteUrl).toBe(publicUrl);
+  });
+
+  it('sync repetido não duplica o registro da foto (idempotente)', async () => {
+    const { db, container } = await setup();
+    const { animalId, animal } = await createOfflineAnimalWithPhoto(container);
+    const useCase = new ProcessSyncQueueUseCase(
+      container.syncQueueRepository,
+      container.animalRepository,
+      undefined,
+      container.remotePhotoStorage
+    );
+    await useCase.execute();
+
+    // Edição posterior reenvia o anúncio: a foto já tem URL e não sobe de novo.
+    const edited = (await container.animalRepository.findById(animalId))!;
+    edited.updateData(UID, { name: 'Mel 2' });
+    await container.animalRepository.saveLocal(edited);
+    await container.syncQueueRepository.enqueue(
+      new SyncAction({ id: newUuid(), operation: 'UPDATE', entityType: 'animal', entityId: animal.id, payload: {} })
+    );
+    const second = await useCase.execute();
+
+    expect(second.successCount).toBe(1);
+    expect(db['animal_photos']).toHaveLength(1);
+  });
+
+  it('falha no upload: a ação falha, o anúncio NÃO vai ao servidor e continua na fila para retry', async () => {
+    const { db, client, container } = await setup();
+    await createOfflineAnimalWithPhoto(container);
+    // Servidor recusa o upload (ex.: sem rede/política).
+    (client.storage as { from: unknown }).from = () => ({
+      upload: async () => ({ data: null, error: { message: 'network down' } }),
+      remove: async () => ({ error: null }),
+      getPublicUrl: () => ({ data: { publicUrl: '' } }),
+    });
+
+    const result = await new ProcessSyncQueueUseCase(
+      container.syncQueueRepository,
+      container.animalRepository,
+      undefined,
+      container.remotePhotoStorage
+    ).execute();
+
+    expect(result).toMatchObject({ successCount: 0, failedCount: 1 });
+    expect(db.animals).toHaveLength(0);
+    expect(await container.syncQueueRepository.listPending()).toHaveLength(1);
+  });
+
+  it('sem backend (modo offline) a sync não tenta enviar fotos', async () => {
+    const container = await loadAppContainer(new InMemorySqliteDriver(), {
+      supabaseClient: null,
+      imageProcessor: new ImageProcessorFake(),
+    });
+    expect(container.remotePhotoStorage).toBeUndefined();
+  });
+
+  it('criação ONLINE também sobe a foto antes do anúncio (sem passar pela fila)', async () => {
+    const { db, client, container } = await setup();
+    const animalId = newUuid();
+    const animal = new Animal({
+      id: animalId,
+      ownerId: UID,
+      name: 'Online',
+      characteristics: new AnimalCharacteristics({ species: 'Cão', size: 'Médio', approximateAge: '2 anos', sex: 'Macho' }),
+      location: new ApproximateLocation({ latitude: -21.5, longitude: -45.4, neighborhood: 'Centro', city: 'Varginha', region: 'MG' }),
+    });
+    const photo = await container.photoStorage.persistLocal(
+      new AnimalPhoto({ id: newUuid(), animalId, localPath: 'file:///cache/c.jpg' })
+    );
+    animal.addPhoto(photo);
+
+    const result = await new CreateAnimalUseCase(
+      container.animalRepository,
+      container.syncQueueRepository,
+      container.unitOfWork
+    ).execute(animal, true);
+
+    expect(result.synced).toBe(true);
+    expect(client.storageObjects.has(`${UID}/${animalId}/${photo.id}.webp`)).toBe(true);
+    expect(db['animal_photos']).toHaveLength(1);
+    const local = await container.animalRepository.findById(animalId);
+    expect(local?.photos[0].remoteUrl).toContain('/animal-photos/');
+  });
+
+  it('trocar a foto apaga o arquivo antigo do bucket; excluir o anúncio apaga os arquivos', async () => {
+    const { client, container } = await setup();
+    const sync = () =>
+      new ProcessSyncQueueUseCase(
+        container.syncQueueRepository,
+        container.animalRepository,
+        undefined,
+        container.remotePhotoStorage
+      ).execute();
+    const { animalId, photo: first } = await createOfflineAnimalWithPhoto(container);
+    await sync();
+    const oldPath = `${UID}/${animalId}/${first.id}.webp`;
+    expect(client.storageObjects.has(oldPath)).toBe(true);
+
+    const second = await container.photoStorage.persistLocal(
+      new AnimalPhoto({ id: newUuid(), animalId, localPath: 'file:///cache/2.jpg' })
+    );
+    await new UpdateAnimalUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork).execute(
+      UID,
+      animalId,
+      { photo: second },
+      false
+    );
+    await sync();
+    expect(client.storageObjects.has(oldPath)).toBe(false);
+    expect(client.storageObjects.has(`${UID}/${animalId}/${second.id}.webp`)).toBe(true);
+
+    await new DeleteAnimalUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork).execute(
+      UID,
+      animalId,
+      false
+    );
+    await sync();
+    expect(client.storageObjects.size).toBe(0);
+  });
+});

+import { loadAppContainer, AppContainer } from '../../../src/infrastructure/composition/loadAppContainer';
+import { InMemorySqliteDriver } from '../../../src/infrastructure/database/sqlite/DatabaseDriver';
+import { getFullSync } from '../../../src/adapters/hooks/useFullSync';
+import { ImageProcessorFake } from '../../../src/application/fakes/ImageProcessorFake';
+import { ToggleFavoriteUseCase } from '../../../src/application/use-cases/ToggleFavoriteUseCase';
+import { RegisterAdoptionInterestUseCase } from '../../../src/application/use-cases/RegisterAdoptionInterestUseCase';
+import { UpdateAnimalUseCase } from '../../../src/application/use-cases/UpdateAnimalUseCase';
+import { MarkAnimalAdoptedUseCase } from '../../../src/application/use-cases/MarkAnimalAdoptedUseCase';
+import { ResolveConflictUseCase } from '../../../src/application/use-cases/ResolveConflictUseCase';
+import { ANIMALS_SYNC_KEY } from '../../../src/application/use-cases/PullRemoteAnimalsUseCase';
+import { FakeSupabaseClient, FakeSupabaseDb, seedFakeDb } from '../../../test-support/SupabaseFake';
+import { newUuid } from '../../../src/domain/services/IdGenerator';
+
+const OWNER = '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111';
+const VISITOR = '22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222';
+const ANIMAL = '33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333';
+
+function row(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
+  return {
+    id: ANIMAL,
+    owner_id: OWNER,
+    name: 'Luna',
+    species: 'Cão',
+    size: 'Pequeno',
+    approximate_age: '2 anos',
+    sex: 'Fêmea',
+    description: 'Dócil',
+    health_notes: null,
+    special_care: null,
+    status: 'AVAILABLE',
+    moderation_status: 'NONE',
+    neighborhood: 'Centro',
+    city: 'Varginha',
+    region: 'MG',
+    latitude: -21.55,
+    longitude: -45.44,
+    created_at: '2026-11-01T10:00:00.000Z',
+    updated_at: '2026-11-01T10:00:00.000Z',
+    version: 1,
+    ...over,
+  };
+}
+
+async function boot(userId: string | null, db: FakeSupabaseDb) {
+  const client = new FakeSupabaseClient({ context: { db, currentUserId: userId, isAdmin: false } });
+  const container = await loadAppContainer(new InMemorySqliteDriver(), {
+    supabaseClient: client,
+    imageProcessor: new ImageProcessorFake(),
+    readFile: async () => new Uint8Array([1]),
+  });
+  return { client, container };
+}
+
+const sync = (c: AppContainer) => getFullSync(c).execute();
+
+describe('Passo 5 — pull do servidor (doc §14.1)', () => {
+  it('traz anúncios de outras pessoas com fotos e grava last_sync_at', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({
+      users: [],
+      animals: [row(), row({ id: newUuid(), name: 'Escondido', moderation_status: 'HIDDEN' })],
+      animal_photos: [
+        { id: newUuid(), animal_id: ANIMAL, remote_url: 'https://cdn/x.webp', is_main: true, created_at: '2026-11-01T10:00:00.000Z' },
+      ],
+    });
+    const { container } = await boot(VISITOR, db);
+
+    const result = await sync(container);
+
+    expect(result.pull).toMatchObject({ fetched: 1, added: 1, updated: 0, skippedPending: 0 });
+    const local = await container.animalRepository.findById(ANIMAL);
+    expect(local?.name).toBe('Luna');
+    expect(local?.photos[0].remoteUrl).toBe('https://cdn/x.webp');
+    const meta = await container.syncMetadataRepository.findByKey(ANIMALS_SYNC_KEY);
+    expect(meta?.lastSuccessAt).toBeDefined();
+  });
+
+  it('atualiza o cache quando o servidor tem versão mais nova e não mexe em anúncio com edição pendente', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] });
+    const { container } = await boot(OWNER, db);
+    await sync(container);
+
+    // Servidor avança; localmente nada pendente → cache é atualizado.
+    db.animals[0] = row({ name: 'Luna Nova', version: 2, updated_at: '2026-11-02T10:00:00.000Z' });
+    const updated = await sync(container);
+    expect(updated.pull).toMatchObject({ updated: 1 });
+    expect((await container.animalRepository.findById(ANIMAL))?.name).toBe('Luna Nova');
+
+    // Edição local pendente (offline) → servidor não sobrescreve.
+    const edit = new UpdateAnimalUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork);
+    await edit.execute(OWNER, ANIMAL, { name: 'Edição local' }, false);
+    db.animals[0] = row({ name: 'Outra', version: 9, updated_at: '2026-11-03T10:00:00.000Z' });
+    const result = await container.syncQueueRepository.listPending();
+    expect(result).toHaveLength(1);
+    const pull = await getFullSync(container).execute();
+    // A fila sobe primeiro; a versão 9 do servidor gera conflito/resolução, nunca perda silenciosa.
+    expect(pull.push.processedCount).toBe(1);
+  });
+
+  it('falha ao ler o servidor não derruba o envio da fila', async () => {
+    const { container, client } = await boot(OWNER, seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [] }));
+    const original = client.from.bind(client);
+    (client as unknown as { from: unknown }).from = (table: string) => {
+      if (table === 'animals') {
+        return { select: () => ({ eq: () => ({ order: () => ({ limit: async () => ({ data: null, error: { message: 'boom' } }) }) }) }) };
+      }
+      return original(table);
+    };
+    const result = await sync(container);
+    expect(result.pullError).toContain('Falha ao ler anúncios do servidor');
+    expect(result.push).toBeDefined();
+  });
+});
+
+describe('Passo 5 — favoritos e interesses na fila', () => {
+  it('favoritar offline enfileira; a sync grava no servidor; desfavoritar remove', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] });
+    const { container } = await boot(VISITOR, db);
+    await sync(container);
+
+    const toggle = new ToggleFavoriteUseCase(container.favoriteRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork);
+    expect(await toggle.execute(VISITOR, ANIMAL)).toBe(true);
+    expect(await container.syncQueueRepository.listPending()).toHaveLength(1);
+
+    await sync(container);
+    expect(db['favorites']).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ user_id: VISITOR, animal_id: ANIMAL })]);
+
+    expect(await toggle.execute(VISITOR, ANIMAL)).toBe(false);
+    await sync(container);
+    expect(db['favorites']).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+
+  it('favoritar e desfavoritar antes de sincronizar se anulam (nada vai ao servidor)', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] });
+    const { container } = await boot(VISITOR, db);
+    const toggle = new ToggleFavoriteUseCase(container.favoriteRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork);
+    await toggle.execute(VISITOR, ANIMAL);
+    await toggle.execute(VISITOR, ANIMAL);
+    expect(await container.syncQueueRepository.listPending()).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+
+  it('anúncio de demonstração (id não-UUID) não entra na fila', async () => {
+    const { container } = await boot(VISITOR, seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [] }));
+    const toggle = new ToggleFavoriteUseCase(container.favoriteRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork);
+    await toggle.execute(VISITOR, 'anim_1');
+    expect(await container.syncQueueRepository.listPending()).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+
+  it('favorito já existente no servidor é idempotente (não vira falha)', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] });
+    db['favorites'] = [{ user_id: VISITOR, animal_id: ANIMAL }];
+    const { container } = await boot(VISITOR, db);
+    const toggle = new ToggleFavoriteUseCase(container.favoriteRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork);
+    await toggle.execute(VISITOR, ANIMAL);
+    const result = await sync(container);
+    expect(result.push).toMatchObject({ successCount: 1, failedCount: 0 });
+    expect(db['favorites']).toHaveLength(1);
+  });
+
+  it('interesse sobe com o contato; só o responsável lê os recebidos', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] });
+    const visitor = await boot(VISITOR, db);
+    await sync(visitor.container);
+
+    const register = new RegisterAdoptionInterestUseCase(
+      visitor.container.interestRepository,
+      visitor.container.syncQueueRepository,
+      visitor.container.unitOfWork
+    );
+    await register.execute(VISITOR, ANIMAL, { name: 'Bia', email: 'bia@x.com', phone: '35999' });
+    await sync(visitor.container);
+    expect(db['adoption_interests']).toEqual([
+      expect.objectContaining({ user_id: VISITOR, animal_id: ANIMAL, contact_name: 'Bia', contact_phone: '35999' }),
+    ]);
+
+    const owner = await boot(OWNER, db);
+    const received = await owner.container.remoteInterests!.listForAnimal(ANIMAL);
+    expect(received).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(received[0]).toMatchObject({ contactName: 'Bia', contactEmail: 'bia@x.com' });
+
+    const stranger = await boot('44444444-4444-4444-8444-444444444444', db);
+    expect(await stranger.container.remoteInterests!.listForAnimal(ANIMAL)).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+});

+describe('Passo 5 — conflitos (doc §9.4, RF23)', () => {
+  async function ownerWithAnimal(db: FakeSupabaseDb) {
+    const ctx = await boot(OWNER, db);
+    await sync(ctx.container); // cache local = versão 1 do servidor
+    return ctx;
+  }
+
+  it('conflito simples (mesma situação): vence a versão mais recente, sem perguntar', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] });
+    const { container } = await ownerWithAnimal(db);
+
+    // Edição local (v2) enquanto o servidor também avançou (v2, mais antiga).
+    const edit = new UpdateAnimalUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork);
+    await edit.execute(OWNER, ANIMAL, { name: 'Local' }, false);
+    db.animals[0] = row({ name: 'Servidor', version: 2, updated_at: '2026-11-01T10:30:00.000Z' });
+
+    const result = await getFullSync(container).execute();
+
+    expect(result.push).toMatchObject({ successCount: 1, conflictCount: 0 });
+    expect(db.animals[0].name).toBe('Local'); // a edição local é a mais recente
+    expect(db.animals[0].version).toBe(3);
+  });
+
+  it('conflito com risco (situação diverge): vira revisão e NÃO é reenviado sozinho', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] });
+    const { container } = await ownerWithAnimal(db);
+
+    const adopt = new MarkAnimalAdoptedUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork);
+    await adopt.execute(OWNER, ANIMAL, false);
+    db.animals[0] = row({ name: 'Editado no servidor', version: 2, updated_at: '2026-11-02T10:00:00.000Z' });
+
+    const first = await getFullSync(container).execute();
+    expect(first.push).toMatchObject({ successCount: 0, conflictCount: 1 });
+    expect(db.animals[0].status).toBe('AVAILABLE'); // nada foi sobrescrito
+
+    const again = await getFullSync(container).execute();
+    expect(again.push.conflictCount).toBe(1); // continua parado, sem retry automático
+    expect(again.push.failedCount).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  it('revisão: "manter minha edição" reenvia e vence; "usar servidor" descarta a local', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] });
+    const { container } = await ownerWithAnimal(db);
+    const adopt = new MarkAnimalAdoptedUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork);
+    await adopt.execute(OWNER, ANIMAL, false);
+    db.animals[0] = row({ name: 'Servidor', version: 2, updated_at: '2026-11-02T10:00:00.000Z' });
+    await getFullSync(container).execute();
+
+    const resolver = new ResolveConflictUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.remoteAnimalSource!, container.syncQueueRepository);
+    const [view] = await resolver.listConflicts();
+    expect(view.local?.status.value).toBe('ADOPTED');
+    expect(view.remote?.name).toBe('Servidor');
+
+    await resolver.keepLocal(view.action.id);
+    const sent = await getFullSync(container).execute();
+    expect(sent.push).toMatchObject({ successCount: 1, conflictCount: 0 });
+    expect(db.animals[0].status).toBe('ADOPTED');
+
+    await expect(resolver.keepLocal(view.action.id)).rejects.toThrow('já foi resolvido');
+  });
+
+  it('revisão: "usar servidor" aplica a versão remota e limpa a fila', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] });
+    const { container } = await ownerWithAnimal(db);
+    const adopt = new MarkAnimalAdoptedUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork);
+    await adopt.execute(OWNER, ANIMAL, false);
+    db.animals[0] = row({ name: 'Servidor', version: 2, updated_at: '2026-11-02T10:00:00.000Z' });
+    await getFullSync(container).execute();
+
+    const resolver = new ResolveConflictUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.remoteAnimalSource!, container.syncQueueRepository);
+    const [view] = await resolver.listConflicts();
+    await resolver.useRemote(view.action.id);
+
+    expect((await container.animalRepository.findById(ANIMAL))?.status.value).toBe('AVAILABLE');
+    expect(await container.syncQueueRepository.listPending()).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+
+  it('anúncio apagado no servidor: revisão oferece recriar ou descartar', async () => {
+    const db = seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] });
+    const { container } = await ownerWithAnimal(db);
+    const edit = new UpdateAnimalUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.syncQueueRepository, container.unitOfWork);
+    await edit.execute(OWNER, ANIMAL, { name: 'Local' }, false);
+    db.animals.length = 0; // removido no servidor
+
+    const first = await getFullSync(container).execute();
+    expect(first.push.conflictCount).toBe(1);
+
+    const resolver = new ResolveConflictUseCase(container.animalRepository, container.remoteAnimalSource!, container.syncQueueRepository);
+    const [view] = await resolver.listConflicts();
+    expect(view.remote).toBeNull();
+    await resolver.keepLocal(view.action.id); // recria
+    const recreated = await getFullSync(container).execute();
+    expect(recreated.push.successCount).toBe(1);
+    expect(db.animals).toHaveLength(1);
+
+    // E descartar: apaga a cópia local.
+    const { container: c2 } = await ownerWithAnimal(seedFakeDb({ users: [], animals: [row()] }));
+    const db2 = (c2.remoteAnimalSource as unknown as { client: { ctx: { db: FakeSupabaseDb } } }).client.ctx.db;
+    await new UpdateAnimalUseCase(c2.animalRepository, c2.syncQueueRepository, c2.unitOfWork).execute(OWNER, ANIMAL, { name: 'X' }, false);
+    db2.animals.length = 0;
+    await getFullSync(c2).execute();
+    const r2 = new ResolveConflictUseCase(c2.animalRepository, c2.remoteAnimalSource!, c2.syncQueueRepository);
+    const [v2] = await r2.listConflicts();
+    await r2.useRemote(v2.action.id);
+    expect(await c2.animalRepository.findById(ANIMAL)).toBeNull();
+  });
+});

+import React from 'react';

+import { makeAnimal, makeLocation } from '../../test-support/fixtures';
+
+const mockMapView = jest.fn();
+jest.mock('react-native-maps', () => {
+  const { View } = require('react-native');
+  const MapView = (props: any) => {
+    mockMapView(props);
+    return <View testID={props.testID}>{props.children}</View>;
+  };
+  const Marker = (props: any) => <View testID={props.testID} {...props} />;
+  const Circle = (props: any) => <View testID="map-circle" {...props} />;
+  return { __esModule: true, default: MapView, Marker, Circle };
+});
+
+import { AnimalMap } from '../../src/adapters/map/AnimalMap';
+
+describe('AnimalMap (módulo de mapa disponível)', () => {
+  const animals = [
+    makeAnimal({ id: 'a', name: 'Luna', location: makeLocation({ latitude: -21.554, longitude: -45.43 }) }),
+    makeAnimal({ id: 'b', name: 'Thor', location: makeLocation({ latitude: -21.6, longitude: -45.5, neighborhood: 'Vila' }) }),
+  ];
+
+  beforeEach(() => mockMapView.mockClear());
+
+  it('renderiza um marcador e um círculo de área aproximada por anúncio, com aviso de privacidade', async () => {
+    const { getByTestId, getAllByTestId } = await render(<AnimalMap animals={animals} />);
+    expect(getByTestId('map-view')).toBeTruthy();
+    expect(getByTestId('map-pin-a').props.coordinate).toEqual({ latitude: -21.55, longitude: -45.43 });
+    expect(getByTestId('map-pin-b').props.title).toBe('Thor');
+    expect(getAllByTestId('map-circle')).toHaveLength(2);
+    expect(getByTestId('map-privacy-note')).toBeTruthy();
+    expect(mockMapView.mock.calls[0][0].initialRegion.latitudeDelta).toBeGreaterThan(0.05);
+  });
+
+  it('tocar no balão do marcador abre o anúncio', async () => {
+    const onSelect = jest.fn();
+    const { getByTestId } = await render(<AnimalMap animals={animals} onSelectAnimal={onSelect} />);
+    await fireEvent(getByTestId('map-pin-b'), 'calloutPress');
+    expect(onSelect).toHaveBeenCalledWith(animals[1]);
+  });
+});

+import { DEFAULT_REGION, markerForAnimal, regionForMarkers } from '../../src/adapters/map/mapRegion';
+import { makeAnimal, makeLocation } from '../../test-support/fixtures';
+
+describe('mapRegion (RF05/RF25)', () => {
+  it('marcador usa SEMPRE a coordenada aproximada, nunca a exata', () => {
+    const animal = makeAnimal({ location: makeLocation({ latitude: -21.55432, longitude: -45.43987 }) });
+    const marker = markerForAnimal(animal);
+    expect(marker.latitude).toBe(-21.55);
+    expect(marker.longitude).toBe(-45.44);
+    expect(marker.latitude).not.toBe(animal.location.latitude);
+    expect(marker.description).toBe('Centro, Varginha');
+    expect(marker.title).toBe('Luna');
+  });
+
+  it('sem anúncios: região padrão do Brasil', () => {
+    expect(regionForMarkers([])).toBe(DEFAULT_REGION);
+  });
+
+  it('um anúncio: centraliza nele com zoom mínimo de cidade', () => {
+    const region = regionForMarkers([markerForAnimal(makeAnimal())]);
+    expect(region.latitude).toBe(-21.55);
+    expect(region.longitude).toBe(-45.43);
+    expect(region.latitudeDelta).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.05);
+  });
+
+  it('vários anúncios: enquadra todos com folga', () => {
+    const a = markerForAnimal(makeAnimal({ id: 'a', location: makeLocation({ latitude: -20, longitude: -45 }) }));
+    const b = markerForAnimal(makeAnimal({ id: 'b', location: makeLocation({ latitude: -22, longitude: -43 }) }));
+    const region = regionForMarkers([a, b]);
+    expect(region.latitude).toBeCloseTo(-21);
+    expect(region.longitude).toBeCloseTo(-44);
+    expect(region.latitudeDelta).toBeCloseTo(2.8);
+    expect(region.longitudeDelta).toBeCloseTo(2.8);
+  });
+});

+import { AnimalPhoto } from '../../src/domain/entities/AnimalPhoto';
+import { ImageConversionError } from '../../src/domain/ports/ImageProcessor';
+import { newUuid } from '../../src/domain/services/IdGenerator';
+import { PhotoStorageFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/PhotoStorageFake';
+import { ImageProcessorFake } from '../../src/application/fakes/ImageProcessorFake';
+import { WebpPhotoStorage } from '../../src/application/photos/WebpPhotoStorage';
+import {
+  ExpoImageManipulatorAdapter,
+  MAX_IMAGE_SIDE,
+  WEBP_QUALITY,
+  fitWithin,
+} from '../../src/infrastructure/device/image/ExpoImageManipulatorAdapter';
+import { base64ToBytes } from '../../src/infrastructure/supabase/storage/base64';
+
+const photo = (localPath?: string, remoteUrl?: string) =>
+  new AnimalPhoto({ id: 'p1', animalId: 'a1', localPath, remoteUrl });
+
+describe('newUuid', () => {
+  it('gera UUID v4 válido e diferente a cada chamada', () => {
+    const re = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-4[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/;
+    const a = newUuid();
+    expect(a).toMatch(re);
+    expect(newUuid()).not.toBe(a);
+    expect(newUuid(() => 0)).toMatch(re);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('WebpPhotoStorage (conversão obrigatória)', () => {
+  it('converte para WebP ANTES de persistir e guarda a foto convertida', async () => {
+    const inner = new PhotoStorageFake();
+    const processor = new ImageProcessorFake();
+    const storage = new WebpPhotoStorage(inner, processor);
+
+    const saved = await storage.persistLocal(photo('file:///cache/cam.jpg'));
+
+    expect(processor.converted).toEqual(['file:///cache/cam.jpg']);
+    expect(saved.localPath).toBe('file:///cache/cam.webp');
+    expect(inner.photos.get('p1')?.localPath).toBe('file:///cache/cam.webp');
+    expect(saved.id).toBe('p1');
+    expect(saved.animalId).toBe('a1');
+  });
+
+  it('falha na conversão: lança erro e não grava nada', async () => {
+    const inner = new PhotoStorageFake();
+    const processor = new ImageProcessorFake();
+    processor.shouldFail = true;
+    const storage = new WebpPhotoStorage(inner, processor);
+
+    await expect(storage.persistLocal(photo('file:///cache/cam.jpg'))).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
+      ImageConversionError
+    );
+    expect(inner.photos.size).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  it('foto só-remota passa direto; demais métodos delegam', async () => {
+    const inner = new PhotoStorageFake();
+    const processor = new ImageProcessorFake();
+    const storage = new WebpPhotoStorage(inner, processor);
+
+    await storage.persistLocal(photo(undefined, 'https://x/y.webp'));
+    expect(processor.converted).toEqual([]);
+
+    await inner.persistLocal(photo('file:///a.webp'));
+    expect(await storage.listPendingUpload()).toHaveLength(1);
+    const uploaded = await storage.uploadPending(photo('file:///a.webp'));
+    expect(uploaded.remoteUrl).toContain('p1');
+    await storage.remove('p1');
+  });
+});
+
+describe('ExpoImageManipulatorAdapter', () => {
+  it('fitWithin reduz mantendo proporção e nunca amplia', () => {
+    expect(fitWithin(4000, 3000)).toEqual({ width: MAX_IMAGE_SIDE, height: 960 });
+    expect(fitWithin(3000, 4000)).toEqual({ width: 960, height: MAX_IMAGE_SIDE });
+    expect(fitWithin(800, 600)).toEqual({ width: 800, height: 600 });
+  });
+
+  function manipulator() {
+    return {
+      manipulateAsync: jest.fn(async (..._args: unknown[]) => ({ uri: 'file:///out.webp' })),
+      SaveFormat: { WEBP: 'webp' },
+    };
+  }
+
+  it('foto grande: redimensiona e salva em WebP com a qualidade definida', async () => {
+    const m = manipulator();
+    const adapter = new ExpoImageManipulatorAdapter({
+      manipulator: m,
+      readSize: async () => ({ width: 4000, height: 3000 }),
+    });
+    expect(await adapter.toWebp('file:///in.jpg')).toBe('file:///out.webp');
+    expect(m.manipulateAsync).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
+      'file:///in.jpg',
+      [{ resize: { width: 1280, height: 960 } }],
+      { compress: WEBP_QUALITY, format: 'webp' }
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('foto pequena ou sem dimensões: converte sem redimensionar', async () => {
+    const small = manipulator();
+    await new ExpoImageManipulatorAdapter({
+      manipulator: small,
+      readSize: async () => ({ width: 500, height: 400 }),
+    }).toWebp('f.jpg');
+    expect(small.manipulateAsync.mock.calls[0][1]).toEqual([]);
+
+    const unknown = manipulator();
+    await new ExpoImageManipulatorAdapter({
+      manipulator: unknown,
+      readSize: async () => null,
+    }).toWebp('f.jpg');
+    expect(unknown.manipulateAsync.mock.calls[0][1]).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it('módulo ausente ou erro do manipulador → ImageConversionError (sem devolver o original)', async () => {
+    await expect(new ExpoImageManipulatorAdapter({ manipulator: null }).toWebp('f.jpg')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
+      ImageConversionError
+    );
+    const broken = {
+      manipulateAsync: jest.fn(async () => {
+        throw new Error('boom');
+      }),
+      SaveFormat: { WEBP: 'webp' },
+    };
+    await expect(
+      new ExpoImageManipulatorAdapter({ manipulator: broken, readSize: async () => null }).toWebp('f.jpg')
+    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ImageConversionError);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('base64ToBytes', () => {
+  it('decodifica com e sem padding', () => {
+    expect(Array.from(base64ToBytes('SGk='))).toEqual([72, 105]);
+    expect(Array.from(base64ToBytes('SGVsbG8'))).toEqual([72, 101, 108, 108, 111]);
+    expect(Array.from(base64ToBytes('AAEC/w=='))).toEqual([0, 1, 2, 255]);
+    expect(base64ToBytes('').length).toBe(0);
+  });
+});

+describe('Cadastro com entrada automática (AuthContext.register)', () => {
+  function Harness({ onDone }: { onDone: (s: { userName?: string }) => void }) {
+    const auth = useAuth();
+    React.useEffect(() => {
+      onDone({ userName: auth.user?.name });
+    }, [auth.user]);
+    return <RegisterScreen onRegister={auth.register} />;
+  }
+
+  async function fillAndSubmit(utils: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof render>>) {
+    await fireEvent.changeText(utils.getByTestId('input-name'), 'Maria Souza');
+    await fireEvent.changeText(utils.getByTestId('input-email'), 'maria@exemplo.com');
+    await fireEvent.changeText(utils.getByTestId('input-pass'), '123456');
+    await fireEvent.changeText(utils.getByTestId('input-confirm-pass'), '123456');
+    await fireEvent.press(utils.getByTestId('btn-register'));
+  }
+
+  it('sem confirmação de e-mail: cria, entra sozinha e guarda a sessão', async () => {
+    const gateway = new AuthGatewayFake();
+    const storage = new SessionStorageFake();
+    const seen: Array<string | undefined> = [];
+    const utils = await render(
+      <AuthProvider authGateway={gateway} sessionStorage={storage}>
+        <Harness onDone={(s) => seen.push(s.userName)} />
+      </AuthProvider>
+    );
+    await fillAndSubmit(utils);
+    await waitFor(() => expect(utils.getByText('Conta criada! Você já está conectado.')).toBeTruthy());
+    expect(seen).toContain('Maria Souza');
+    expect(await storage.getSessionToken()).toMatch(/^fake_token_usr_/);
+  });
+
+  it('com confirmação de e-mail: não entra e orienta a confirmar', async () => {
+    const gateway = new AuthGatewayFake();
+    const original = gateway.register.bind(gateway);
+    gateway.register = async (input) => ({ ...(await original(input)), needsEmailConfirmation: true });
+    const storage = new SessionStorageFake();
+    const utils = await render(
+      <AuthProvider authGateway={gateway} sessionStorage={storage}>
+        <Harness onDone={() => {}} />
+      </AuthProvider>
+    );
+    await fillAndSubmit(utils);
+    await waitFor(() =>
+      expect(utils.getByText('Cadastro realizado! Confirme seu e-mail e depois faça login.')).toBeTruthy()
+    );
+    expect(await storage.getSessionToken()).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('se a entrada automática falhar, a conta existe e a pessoa é mandada ao login', async () => {
+    const gateway = new AuthGatewayFake();
+    gateway.login = jest.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error('falhou'));
+    const utils = await render(
+      <AuthProvider authGateway={gateway} sessionStorage={new SessionStorageFake()}>
+        <Harness onDone={() => {}} />
+      </AuthProvider>
+    );
+    await fillAndSubmit(utils);
+    await waitFor(() => expect(utils.getByText('Cadastro realizado com sucesso! Faça login.')).toBeTruthy());
+  });
+});
+
+describe('AuthContext.updateProfile / perfil local', () => {
+  function Probe({ capture }: { capture: (a: ReturnType<typeof useAuth>) => void }) {
+    capture(useAuth());
+    return <Text>ok</Text>;
+  }
+
+  it('exige login para editar', async () => {
+    let auth!: ReturnType<typeof useAuth>;
+    await render(
+      <AuthProvider authGateway={new AuthGatewayFake()} sessionStorage={new SessionStorageFake()}>
+        <Probe capture={(a) => (auth = a)} />
+      </AuthProvider>
+    );
+    await expect(auth.updateProfile({ bio: 'x' })).rejects.toThrow('Faça login para editar o perfil.');
+  });
+
+  it('edição local vence o servidor ao reentrar (não perde edição feita offline)', async () => {
+    const gateway = new AuthGatewayFake();
+    const repo = new UserRepositoryFake();
+    const storage = new SessionStorageFake();
+    await repo.save(gateway.mockUsers[0].withProfile({ bio: 'bio local', interests: ['Gatos'] }));
+
+    let auth!: ReturnType<typeof useAuth>;
+    await render(
+      <AuthProvider authGateway={gateway} sessionStorage={storage} userRepository={repo}>
+        <Probe capture={(a) => (auth = a)} />
+      </AuthProvider>
+    );
+    await waitFor(() => expect(auth.isLoading).toBe(false));
+    const user = await auth.login('ana@exemplo.com', 'senha');
+    expect(user.bio).toBe('bio local');
+    expect(user.interests).toEqual(['Gatos']);
+
+    // `auth` é reatribuído a cada render: espera o estado com o usuário logado.
+    await waitFor(() => expect(auth.user?.id).toBe('usr_1'));
+    const saved = await auth.updateProfile({ bio: 'novo' });
+    expect(saved.user.bio).toBe('novo');
+    expect((await repo.findById('usr_1'))?.bio).toBe('novo');
+  });
+});

+import React from 'react';
+import { render, fireEvent, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react-native';
+import { ModerationScreen } from '../../src/adapters/screens/ModerationScreen';
+import { ModerateAnimalUseCase } from '../../src/application/use-cases/ModerateAnimalUseCase';
+import { ModerationGateway, ModerationEntry } from '../../src/domain/ports/ModerationGateway';
+import { User } from '../../src/domain/entities/User';
